Summary 249
Summary
[Title] 2016 Global Innovation Scoreboard
ㆍProject leader: Ki Kook Kim
ㆍParticipants: Hee Jong Kang · Tae Yang Kim · Saymin Lee · Eun Sun Jo
As a follow-up report of 2015 Global Innovation Scoreboard (2015 GIS), 2016
Global Innovation Scoreboard (2016 GIS) applied same ecosystem model and
index system for connectivity with previous year’s. In addition, the foregoing has
a signification in examining of index system through comparison with the first
year’s report. it consists of five major classifications, namely, actor, activity,
environment, market and coordination, and 12 medium classification and 86
sub-indexes. According to the characteristic s of sub-indexes, they were classified
into healthiness(Present capability), di versity(Sustainable capability), dynamics
(Development capability). Using the maximum-minimum method, sub-indexes
were standardized in unit. The Global Innovation Index(GII) was computed
through the comprehensive mean of every index and each ecosystem
component(actor, activity, environment, ma rket, coordination) was also measured
through mean. For such components, characteristics-specific evaluation (healthiness,
diversity, dynamic) was performed along with component-specific evaluations. As
for the index verification, robustness verification was conducted for examining
stability through the fluctuation of year-specific ranking (2007-2015) of 2016
GIS. The result was stably evaluated by comparing with the IMD World
Competitiveness Yearbook and the WEF National Competitiveness Evaluation.
While showing strength in actor and market, Korea showed vulnerability in
activity. The subject and market capacities were found to be high as a result of
the outcome achieved in the small concentrated market by utilizing a high level
of quantitative input of a small number of innovation actors. The activity and
coordination vulnerability was shown as a result of the issues such as lack of
participation by the majority of actors in innovation activity, insufficient

===== PDF_PAGE 274 =====
250 2016 글로벌 혁신 스코어보드
connection between the majority and minority of actor and insufficient scope and
level of governmental coordination for correction.
Ranked 17th in the ecosystem model-based 2016 Global Innovation Index
(2016 GII), Korea moved up to one spot as compared to the result of 2015
Global Innovation Index (2015 GII). As for the capability according to the
classification by the elements of ecosyst em, Korea ranked 9th in actor, 29th in
activity, 16th in environment, 10th in market and 22nd in coordination
respectively. While strong in the aspects of actor and market similar to the result
of the first year’s report, Korea remained weak in the activity and coordination.
In dynamic, innovation hindrance factor was relatively lesser but it lacked indirect
innovation activity. In diversity, type of industry and technology was found to be
diverse and the balance of industry and academia was found to be high. However,
government support and number of academic areas were found to be relatively
lesser and the patent market showed an aspect of excessive concentration around
particular countries in patent development. While Healthiness showed a high level
of competitiveness in the aspect of stability showing quantitative scale, it showed
a low level of competitiveness in low ranking in the aspect of efficiency,
interaction, connectedness and control action.
When viewing changes in rankings through the comparison of 2015 GIS and
2016 GIS, Korea moved up in the actor (1 spot), market (3 spots) and
coordination (3 spots), remained unchanged in the environment, but dropped in
the activity (3 spots). With respect to th e moving up in rankings of actor, market
and coordination, healthiness of actor wa s influenced by 1) improved capability
of the government research institutes 2) improved dynamics capability of market
and 3) improved healthiness of coordination and diversity capability. Decline of
the ranking of environment was caused by very low healthiness capability in spite
of high dynamics capability. In the comparison of innovation capability by the
characteristics of Korea, dynamics was evaluated as 13th, diversity was in
medium class as 17th and healthiness ranked 21st.
2016 GII aimed to understand factors changing innovation capability of main
countries (Korea, USA, China, Japan, Switz erland, Austria) not dealt with in the
2015 GIS. The aforementioned is consid ered significant in the way that it

===== PDF_PAGE 275 =====
Summary 251
understands strength·weakness of innovatio n capability of main countries through
the changes in rankings of the elemen ts of ecosystem. 2016 GIS especially
concentrated on understanding the factors changing innovation capability of
companies of main countries including Korea. 2016 GIS discussed ways to
strengthen innovation capability of Korean companies by considering influential
factors other than the elements of ecosyste m as well the influential factors of the
elements of ecosystem.
In regards to the capacity of Korea exam ined by comparing with that of other
countries from the ecosystem perspectiv e, it showed an aspect of imbalance
internally while showing healthiness externally(quantitative scale). The significance
of this report can be found in that it examined the aspect of such imbalance
through numerical compar ison according to the ecosystem components. In
addition, it has examined the current situ ation of the innovation capacity of Korea
by examining the streng ths and weaknesses accord ing to each ecosystem
component characteristic and according po licy and implications were suggested.

우리 연구원은 과학기술정책 분야의 연구를 전문적으로 수행하는 정부출연연구기관으로서
과학기술정책 연구 분야에 관심 있는 분들이 연구 성과물을 널리 이용할 수 있도록 아래와
같이 선별 판매를 하고 있습니다.
